<Z_{\mathbf{g}}^{\text{{cft}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};\mathbf{x}\right)>_{\mathcal{M}}:=\mathcal{Z}_{\mathbf{g}}^{\text{{cft}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right),

(187)

Here, we describe the two dimensional surface (string world sheet)
in terms of a 2- torus
\Sigma=\mathbb{S}_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}_{t_{E}}^{1}
, where
\mathbb{S}_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}}^{1}
parameterizes space while
\mathbb{S}_{t_{E}}^{1}
represents the Euclidean time.

Averaged partition function of Narain CFTs

The genus-one partition function
Z_{{\small D}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};\mathbf{x}\right)
of the sigma-model with target space
\mathbb{T}^{{\small D}}
(here
D=r
) is a function of
\left(\mathbf{1}\right)
the
modular parameters
\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)
of the 2-torus and
\left(\mathbf{2}\right)
the coordinates
\mathbf{x}=\left(x_{i}^{k}\right)
of the moduli space
\mathcal{M}_{{\small D}}.
It takes the form

Z_{{\small D}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};\mathbf{x}\right)=\frac{\Theta_{{\small D}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};\mathbf{x}\right)}{\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{2{\small D}}}

(188)

where
\Theta_{{\small D}}
is the Siegel-Narain theta function obeying the
differential equation

\left(\Delta_{{\small H}}-D{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}}-\Delta_{\mathcal{M}_{{\small D}}}\right)\Theta_{{\small D}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};\mathbf{x}\right)=0

(189)

and the asymptotic property
\lim_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\rightarrow\infty}\Theta_{{\small D}}=1.

{}_{2}^{\mathbf{g}}
theory of

and where

with

reading explicitly as (

)

By introducing the change of variables

and

with

as

where we have set

. By substituting in (

Focussing on the Siegel-Narain theta function classified by

\displaystyle=

In the large limit of

(203)

following from the asymptotic

Using (

), we can also deduce the mean value

and reading as follows

From eq(

), we see that the above
F_{{\small D}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)
verifies the differential equation

\left(\Delta_{{\small H}}-D{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}\frac{\partial}{\partial{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}}\right)F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)=0

(206)

In this relation, the laplacian
\Delta_{{\small H}}
on the half plane is
defined by
-{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}^{2}(\partial_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}}^{2}+\partial_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}^{2}).
Moreover, due to
F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)/\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{2{\small D}}
being modular invariant,
the averaged
F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)

transforms under modular transformations by scaling factors with weights
\left(u,v\right)=\left(D/2,D/2\right)
. Indeed, by performing the change

{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\rightarrow{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}^{\prime}=\frac{a{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+b}{c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d}

(207)

with integers
\left(a,b,c,d\right)
constrained as

and using

\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}^{\prime}\right)\right|=\left|c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d\right|^{1/2}\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|

(208)

we have the following scaling

F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}^{\prime},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}^{\prime}\right)=\left(c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d\right)^{\frac{D}{2}}\left(c\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}+d\right)^{\frac{D}{2}}F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)

(209)

Furthermore, upon imposing the modular transformation
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{\prime}={{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}/\left|c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d\right|^{2},
it follows
that the function
W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right):={{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}^{D/2}F_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)
is modular invariant and behaves for large values of
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}
like

\frac{1}{vol(\mathcal{M}_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}})}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{{\small D}/2}

(210)

due to (
203
). This volume is function of the Cartan matrix
K_{\mathbf{g}}.
By substituting into (
206
) and using
\Delta_{{\small H}}({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}^{-s}W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}})=-s(s-1){{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}^{-s}W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}
with

we end up with the
following differential equation

\left[\Delta_{H}+s(s-1)\right]W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)=0\qquad,\qquad s=D/2

(211)

showing that
W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}
is an eigen function of

with eigenvalue
-s(s-1).
Candidates of such eigen functions
are given by the (real analytic) Eisenstein series defined as follows

,
12
]

E_{s}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)=\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{c,d}\frac{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{{\small 2}}^{s}}{\left|c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d\right|^{2s}}\qquad,\qquad{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}=\mathop{\mathrm{I}m}\nolimits{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}

(212)

with coprime integers
\left(c,d\right)\simeq\left(-c,-d\right).
This
series can be also imagined in terms of sum is over all modular images of
the function
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{s};
and as such it can defined as follows

E_{s}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)=\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}\in SL_{2}/\Pi}\mathop{\mathrm{I}m}\nolimits\left(\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)^{s}

(213)

showing that it is modular invariant. In this writing, the
\textstyle\mathchar 269

belongs to the coset group
\mathrm{SL}_{2}/\Pi_{1}
with generic
transformations as follows

\displaystyle SL(2,\mathbb{Z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}a&b\\
c&d\end{array}\right),\text{ }ad-bc=1\right\}

\displaystyle\Pi(1,\mathbb{Z})

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\left\{\boldsymbol{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}}_{n}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}1&n\\
0&1\end{array}\right),\text{ \qquad}n\in\mathbb{Z}\right\}

Recall that under these group transformations, the imaginary part
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}=\mathop{\mathrm{I}m}\nolimits{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}
is invariant under the translations generated by
\boldsymbol{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}}_{1}
while it is mapped into
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}/\left|c{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}+d\right|^{2}
under the S application. Notice also that the
series (
212
) converges for
\mathop{\mathrm{R}e}\nolimits s>1
corresponding
in our case to
D>2

[
1
]
.

In sum, we have the following features:

\left(\mathbf{i}\right)

The function
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{s}
is an
eigenfunction of the Laplacian
\Delta_{H}
with eigenvalue
-s(s-1)
; the
same is valid for its modular images. So, the
\lim_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\rightarrow\infty}

E_{s}={{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{s}
correspond to the
identity element of the
\mathrm{SL}\left(2,\mathbb{Z}\right)
group (
a=d=1
). As such
E_{{\small D}/2}
satisfies all of the desired properties
of
W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}.

\left(\mathbf{ii}\right)

The functions
E_{{\small D}/2}
and
W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}
can be superimposed; their
difference

W_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}}-c_{\mathbf{g}}E_{{\small D}/2}

(220)

grows at infinity slowly than
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{D/2}
; and hence must vanish

[
1
]
. In this relation, we have

c_{\mathbf{g}}=\frac{1}{vol(\mathcal{M}_{\mathbf{g}}^{({\small D,D)}})}

(221)

Gravitational dual

The 3D bulk partition function
Z_{\text{{bulk}}}
of the
gravitational dual
[
53
]
of the averaged genus-one partition
function
Z^{\text{{cft}}}
of Narain CFT
2
with target space
\mathbb{T}^{d}
is expressed as a sum over handlebodies Y as follows

Z_{\text{{bulk}}}=\sum_{\text{handlebadies {Y}}}Z_{\text{{cs}}}^{\text{{y}}}

(222)

Here Y is the thermal AdS
3
given by
\mathcal{D}\times\mathbb{S}_{t}^{1}
where
\mathcal{D}
is a disc with boundary
\mathbb{S}_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}}^{1}
that we concisely write as

Y=\mathcal{D}\times\mathbb{S}_{t}^{1}\qquad,\qquad\partial Y=\mathbb{S}_{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}_{t}^{1}

(223)

and the
Z_{\text{{cs}}}^{\text{{y}}}
is the partition
function of
U(1)^{d}\times U(1)^{d}
Chern-Simons fields on Y. In this
regard, recall that in 3D Einstein gravity possesses no physical
bulk excitations other than the boundary gravitons first described
by Brown and Henneaux
[
40
]
. The path integral of the Chern-Simons field
on
\mathcal{D}\times\mathbb{S}_{t}^{1}
simply equals the partition
function of the Brown-Henneaux modes. Thus, the bulk
Z_{\text{{cs}}}^{\text{{y}}}
is given by the thermal partition
function of boundary gravitons composites of the D boundary photons
whose partition function is

Z_{\text{{cs}}}^{\text{{y}}}=\frac{1}{\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{2d}}=q^{-\frac{2d}{24}}\mathop{\displaystyle\prod}\limits_{n=1}^{\infty}\frac{1}{\left|1-q^{n}\right|^{2d}}

(224)

This matches the vacuum character of d copies of the
U(1)\times U(1)
current algebra
[
49
]
. This expression can be verified in a
direct bulk computation in
U(1)^{2d}
Chern-Simons theory, just as in the
gravity case.

To get the full partition function
Z_{\text{{bulk}}},
we need to
sum over all handlebodies,

Z_{\text{{bulk}}}=\sum_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}\in\Pi\backslash SL}_{2}}\frac{1}{\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left(\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{2d}}

(225)

By factorising
\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{-2d}
like
the product
({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{-d/2}\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{-2d})\times{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{d/2}
with modular invariant
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{-d/2}\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{-2d}
, and
thinking about the factor
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{d/2}
in terms of
E_{d/2},
we end up
with the following expression of the bulk partition function

Z_{\text{{bulk}}}^{\mathbf{g}}=\frac{c_{\mathbf{g}}E_{d/2}}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{d/2}\left|{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 273$}}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}\right)\right|^{2d}}

(226)

with
c_{\mathbf{g}}
as in (
221
).

Appendix B
Root and weight lattices of Lie algebras
\mathbf{g}

In this appendix, we provide a summary of useful tools on the root

\Lambda_{r}
and the weight
\Lambda_{w}
lattices of finite dimensional
Lie algebras
\mathbf{g}
. We also describe the congruency classes
of the coset
\Lambda_{w}/\Lambda_{r}
. These tools play an
integral role in the main text.

Lattices
\Lambda_{r}
and
\Lambda_{weight}

Root lattice of a finite dimensional Lie algebra
\Lambda_{root}:=\Lambda_{r}^{\mathbf{g}}
and its weight
\Lambda_{weight}:=\Lambda_{w}^{\mathbf{g}}
are generated by the simple roots
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{i}
and
fundamental weights
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{i}
. Sites
r
n

in the lattice
\Lambda_{r}^{\mathbf{g}}
are given by the
integral expansion
\sum n^{i}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{i}.
Similarly, sites
k
w
in the weight lattice are given by
\sum w_{i}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{i}
. Formally, we have

\Lambda_{r}^{\mathbf{g}}

=

\mathbb{Z}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{1}+...+\mathbb{Z}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{r}

\Lambda_{w}^{\mathbf{g}}

=

\mathbb{Z}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}_{1}+...+\mathbb{Z}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}_{r}

(227)

Because of the relationship
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{i}=K_{ij}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{j}
between simple roots and fundamental weights, we have the inclusion
\Lambda_{r}^{\mathbf{g}}\subset\Lambda_{w}^{\mathbf{g}}
. Putting into
\mathbf{r}_{\mathbf{n}}=\sum_{i}n^{i}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{i}
, we end up with
the following expansion

\mathbf{r}_{\mathbf{n}}=\sum_{i,j=1}^{r}\left(K_{ij}n^{i}\right)\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{j}=\sum_{j=1}^{r}m_{j}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{j}

(228)

with
m_{j}=\sum_{i}n^{i}K_{ij}.
Moreover, seen that the number of
congruency classes is equal to the number of distinct elements of the coset
\Gamma:=\Lambda_{w}^{\mathbf{g}}/\Lambda_{r}^{\mathbf{g}},
it follows
that the factor
\Gamma
is a finite group with representative element
\mathbf{\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 285$}}}}}=\sum\bar{v}^{i}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}_{i}
that has a
definite expression for each algebra. As such, given a weight
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}=\sum w_{i}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}^{i},
the congruency classes are
defined as follows

\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}.\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 285$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 285$}}}}}=\sum_{i=1}^{r}w_{i}\bar{v}^{i}\qquad\mathop{\mathrm{m}od}\nolimits\left|\Gamma\right|

(229)

In this regards, recall that the order
\left|\Gamma\right|
of
the coset group
\Gamma
is known to be equal to the determinant of the
Cartan matrix
K_{ij},
that is
\det K=\left|\Gamma\right|
.

Appendix C
Metric of
\mathcal{M}_{\mathbf{g}}
and average of
\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}
function

In this appendix, we provide detailed derivations for the results
reported in the main text. First, we calculate the metric
\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}
of the moduli space. Then, we evaluate the
Siegel-Narain theta function
\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]
at
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}=0
; this permits to identify the value of
the constant c
g
and the dependence in
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}
of the
average
<\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}>_{\mathcal{M}}.

String field in the Cartan basis

Here we construct the field action S
\left[X\right]
of a compactified
string field
X\left(t,{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\right)
on a circle that is manifestly
invariant under T-duality. This symmetry exchanges
\mathbb{S}^{1}
with
radius
R
into a dual circle
\mathbb{\tilde{S}}^{1}
with radius
1/R
;
thus permitting to think about the action of T on
\mathbb{S}^{1}
in terms
of the duplication
\mathbb{S}^{1}\times\mathbb{\tilde{S}}^{1}
and the
permutation

T:\mathbb{S}^{1}\times\mathbb{\tilde{S}}^{1}\qquad\rightarrow\qquad\mathbb{\tilde{S}}^{1}\times\mathbb{S}^{1}

with matrix representation

T=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}0&1\\
1&0\end{array}\right)\qquad,\qquad T^{2}=I_{id}

(239)

Below, we show that this T-invariant action S
\left[X\right]
, from now on denoted like
S\left[\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right],
has the
form

S_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left[\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right]\simeq\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{\mathbb{R}^{1,1}}dtd{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\text{ }\sum_{\text{{a},{b}=1}}^{2}\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}^{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right)

(240)

with the doublet
\Phi^{\text{{a}}}
given by (
U,V
) as
shown below and the metric
\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}
given by

\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}^{\mathbf{su}_{2}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\frac{1}{2R^{2}}&0\\
0&2R^{2}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}2x^{2}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{2x^{2}}\end{array}\right)

(241)

where we have set
x=1/(2R).
The T-duality exchanges the circles
\mathbb{S}^{1}
and
\mathbb{\tilde{S}}^{1}
; it acts like
T:\sqrt{2}R\rightarrow 1/(\sqrt{2}R)
and maps the field components like
U\leftrightarrow V.

In our setting, parameterised by the su(2) simple root
\textstyle\mathchar 267
and its fundamental weight
\textstyle\mathchar 277
, the above
T-duality can be imagined in terms of ”scaled” simple root
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}\left(x\right)
and ”scaled” fundamental weight
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}\left(x\right)
. They are given by

\begin{tabular}[]{lll}$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}$&$=$&$x\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}$\\
$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}$&$=$&$\frac{1}{x}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}$\end{tabular}\qquad,\qquad\begin{tabular}[]{lll}$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}$&$=$&2$x^{2}$\\
$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}$&$=$&$\frac{1}{2x^{2}}$\end{tabular}\qquad,\qquad\begin{tabular}[]{lll}$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}.{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}$&$=$&1\\
$\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}$&$=$&$1$\end{tabular}

(242)

with

that can be decomposed as the

In the basis (

where we have used the parametrisation (

). We get

\boldsymbol{p}_{1}=n\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}:}=p\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}\qquad,\qquad\boldsymbol{p}_{2}=w\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}:}=q\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}

exchanged under the permutation
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}\leftrightarrow\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}

From these relationships, we deduce the canonical fields

V\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}

(249)

and well as their gradients

=

\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{L}+\boldsymbol{X}_{R}\right)

\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}V\right)

=

\frac{1}{\sqrt{2}}\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\left(\boldsymbol{X}_{L}-\boldsymbol{X}_{R}\right)

(250)

The corresponding two dimensional field action
S\left[U,V\right]

reads as follows:

S_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left[U,V\right]\simeq\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{\mathbb{R}^{1,1}}dtd{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\text{ }\left[\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}U\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}U\right)+\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}V\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}V\right)\right]

(251)

and splits like
S\left[U\right]+S\left[V\right]
with

\displaystyle S\left[U\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{\mathbb{R}^{1,1}}dtd{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\text{ }\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}U\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}U\right)

(252)

\displaystyle S\left[V\right]

\displaystyle\simeq

\displaystyle\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{\mathbb{R}^{1,1}}dtd{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}V\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}V\right)

(253)

indicating in turn that the metric
ds_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}^{2}=\left(d{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 278$}}}\right)^{2}
of the moduli subspace
\left]0,\infty\right[
is
given by
\left(d{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 278$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 278$}}}\right)^{2}\simeq\left(dR/R\right)^{2}.
By
setting
\Phi^{\text{{a}}}=\left(U,V\right),
we can also rewrite
this action as follows

S_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left[U,V\right]\simeq\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{\mathbb{R}^{1,1}}dtd{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 283$}}}\text{ }\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}\left(\partial_{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right)\left(\partial^{\mathrm{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 269$}}}}}\Phi^{\text{{a}}}\right)

(254)

with

\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}^{\mathbf{su}_{2}}=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}&0\\
0&\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}\end{array}\right)=\left(\begin{array}[]{cc}2x^{2}&0\\
0&\frac{1}{2x^{2}}\end{array}\right)

(255)

This construction extends straightforwardly to higher dimensional NCFT
g
s with Lie algebra
g
and metric
\mathcal{G}_{\text{{ab}}}^{\mathbf{g}}
. For these theories, the metric
ds_{\mathbf{g}}^{2}
has the form
\left(\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}^{2}.}d\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}\right)^{2}.

Computing
\Theta_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]
and
\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

Here, we study properties of the function
\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}};R\right]
and
F_{\mathbf{g}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)

of eq(
68
) by considering first the case
\mathrm{su}_{2}
and turn
after to generic Lie algebras
\mathbf{g}
.

A)
Higher ranks NCFT
{}_{\mathbf{su}_{2}}^{(1,1)}

Recall that the Siegel-Narain theta function is given by

\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{n,w}e^{2i{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}nw}\exp\left[-{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\left(2x^{2}n^{2}+\frac{1}{2x^{2}}w^{2}\right)\right]

(256)

Its ”averaged” function
F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}},\bar{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}}\right)

in terms of the variable x can be formulated as:

F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)\sim\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{0}^{\infty}\frac{dx}{x}\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

(257)

where we set the measure
dx^{l}/x^{l}=ldx/x
with
l=\pm 1,
and
x=1/(2R).
To compute
F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}
and
later
F_{\mathbf{g}}
, we start from eq(
58
) and calculate
the double sum of
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 293$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 293$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 293$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 293$}}}_{n,w}^{\mathbf{su}_{2}}\left(x;{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)
. Given that
\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}
is invariant
by translation in the
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}
direction; i.e:
\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}+1\right]=\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}\right],
one exploits this symmetry to set
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}=0
. This feature
also manifests via the contribution in
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}
in (
256
)
which is given by the phase factor e
{}^{2i{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}nw}=(-)^{2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}}

indicating that we have

\Theta_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1},{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=(-)^{2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

(258)

with

{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{n,w}\exp\left[-{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\left(2x^{2}n^{2}+\frac{1}{2x^{2}}w^{2}\right)\right]

In what follows, we set
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{1}
to zero and focus on computing
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]
and
F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right).
In this regard, notice that the above
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]
factorises like

{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]\times\bar{W}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

with factors
\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}
and
\bar{W}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}

as given by the series

\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

=

\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{-\left(2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}x^{2}\right)n^{2}}

\bar{W}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

=

\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{w\in\mathbb{Z}}e^{-\frac{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}{2x^{2}}w^{2}}

(259)

converging to

\displaystyle\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}2x^{2}}}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}\frac{1}{\left|x\right|}

(260)

\displaystyle\bar{W}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2x^{2}}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}=\sqrt{\frac{2}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}\left|x\right|

(261)

From these formulas, we deduce the following features:

•

The
{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]
converges
to the value

{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 291$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\bar{K}\left(x\right)\bar{W}\left(x\right)=\frac{1}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}

(262)

manifestly invariant under T-transformation. It is independent of the
modulus
x
. Substituting into (
257
), we find that
F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)
diverge

F_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)=\frac{1}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{0}^{\infty}\left(\log x\right)dx=\infty

(263)

Notice also that

\displaystyle{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 276$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 276$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 276$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 276$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{1}^{\infty}\frac{dx}{x}\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

(264)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{1}{2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{1}^{\infty}\frac{dx}{x^{2}}=\sqrt{\frac{1}{2{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}

and

\displaystyle{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 289$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 289$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 289$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 289$}}}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\right)

\displaystyle:

\displaystyle=\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{1}^{\infty}\frac{dx}{x}W_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]

(265)

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\sqrt{\frac{2}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}\displaystyle\int\nolimits_{1}^{\infty}dx=\infty

•

The functions
\bar{K}\left(x\right)
and
\bar{W}\left(x\right)

read in terms of the scaled root
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}=x\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 267$}}}}
and the
scaled weight
\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}=\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 277$}}}}/x
as follows

\bar{K}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\sqrt{\frac{\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 287$}}}}^{2}}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}\qquad,\qquad\bar{W}_{\mathrm{su}_{2}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\sqrt{\frac{\mathbf{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 268$}}}}^{2}}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}}}

exchangeable under T-duality.

B) Higher ranks NCFT
{}_{\mathbf{g}}^{(r,r)}

From the above su(2) set up, we can draw the generalisation
towards NCFT
{}_{\mathrm{g}}^{(r,r)}
s labeled by higher dimensional Lie
algebras. We have

\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};x\right]=\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{n,w}\exp\left[-{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\left(n^{i}\mathcal{K}_{ij}^{\mathbf{g}}n^{i}+w_{i}\mathcal{\tilde{K}}_{\mathbf{g}}^{ij}w_{j}\right)\right]

(266)

with

\mathcal{K}_{ij}^{\mathbf{g}}=x_{i}^{k}K_{kl}^{\mathbf{g}}x_{j}^{l}\qquad,\qquad\mathcal{\tilde{K}}_{\mathbf{g}}^{ij}=y_{k}^{i}K_{\mathbf{g}}^{kl}y_{l}^{j}

(267)

It factorises
\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]=\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]\times\bar{W}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]
with factors
\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}
and

\bar{W}_{\mathbf{g}}
given by the series

\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

=

\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{n\in\mathbb{Z}}\exp\left[-n^{i}\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\mathcal{K}_{ij}^{\mathbf{g}}\right)n^{i}\right]

\bar{W}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

\mathop{\displaystyle\sum}\limits_{w\in\mathbb{Z}}\exp\left[-w_{i}({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\mathcal{\tilde{K}}_{\mathbf{g}}^{ij})w_{j}\right]

(268)

converging to

\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]=\sqrt{\frac{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}^{r}}{\det\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\mathcal{K}_{ij}^{\mathbf{g}}\right)}}\qquad,\qquad\bar{W}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]=\sqrt{\frac{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}^{r}}{\det\left({{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 281$}}}{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}\mathcal{\tilde{K}}_{\mathbf{g}}^{ij}\right)}}

(269)

By using the relationships (

), we also have

\displaystyle\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\bar{W}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{\left(\det K_{ij}^{\mathbf{g}}\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}}{{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2}^{r/2}}\times\left|\det x_{i}^{k}\right|

•

\Theta_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

converges to the value

manifestly invariant under T-duality. It is independent of the modulus

. Substituting into (

diverges

(271)

•

The functions
\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

and

read in terms
of the scaled root

as follows

\displaystyle\bar{K}_{\mathbf{g}}\left[{{}\mathchoice{\mbox{\boldmath$\displaystyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\textstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}{\mbox{\boldmath$\scriptscriptstyle\mathchar 284$}}}_{2};\mathbf{x}\right]

\displaystyle=

(272)
